===== CURRENT FRONTEND ROUTER =====
import {
  Navigate,
  Route,
  Routes,
} from "react-router-dom";

import OperationsCenterGovernancePage from "../app/operations-center/governance/page";
import PrivacyGovernancePage from "../app/privacy-governance/page";
import FunctionalityHealthPage from "../app/operations-center/functionality-health/page";
import EmailWorkflowsPage from "../app/operations-center/email-workflows/page";
import RiskIntelligencePage from "../app/operations-center/compliance/cmp/risk-intelligence/risk-coverage/page";

export default function AppRouter() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      {/* Operations Center Governance */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/governance"
        element={<OperationsCenterGovernancePage />}
      />

      {/* Operations Center Functionality Health */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/functionality-health"
        element={<FunctionalityHealthPage />}
      />

      {/* Operations Center Email Workflows */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/email-workflows"
        element={<EmailWorkflowsPage />}
      />

      {/* Compliance â†’ CMP â†’ Risk Intelligence â†’ Risk Coverage */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/compliance/cmp/risk-intelligence/risk-coverage"
        element={<RiskIntelligencePage />}
      />

      {/* Backward-compatible redirect for the initial Risk Intelligence URL */}
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/risk-intelligence"
        element={
          <Navigate
            to="/operations-center/compliance/cmp/risk-intelligence/risk-coverage"
            replace
          />
        }
      />

      {/* Privacy Governance dashboard */}
      <Route
        path="/privacy-governance"
        element={<PrivacyGovernancePage />}
      />

      {/* Privacy Governance specific case */}
      <Route
        path="/privacy-governance/:caseId"
        element={<PrivacyGovernancePage />}
      />

      {/* Application root */}
      <Route
        path="/"
        element={
          <Navigate
            to="/operations-center/governance"
            replace
          />
        }
      />

      {/* Unknown routes */}
      <Route
        path="*"
        element={
          <Navigate
            to="/operations-center/governance"
            replace
          />
        }
      />
    </Routes>
  );
}

import React from "react";
import ReactDOM from "react-dom/client";
import { BrowserRouter } from "react-router-dom";

import AppRouter from "./router";

const rootElement = document.getElementById("root");

if (!rootElement) {
  throw new Error(
    "EasyChatBot React root element was not found.",
  );
}

ReactDOM.createRoot(rootElement).render(
  <React.StrictMode>
    <BrowserRouter>
      <AppRouter />
    </BrowserRouter>
  </React.StrictMode>,
);

const NAVIGATION_ITEMS = [

    label: "Overview",
    href: "/operations-center",

    label: "Providers",
    href: "/operations-center/providers",

  },

    label: "Agents",
    href: "/operations-center/agents",
  },

    label: "Reports",
    href: "/operations-center/reports",
  },
  {
    label: "Settings",
    href: "/operations-center/settings",
  },
] as const;

const OPERATIONS_CONTROL_ITEMS = [
  {
    label: "Functionality Health",
    href: "/operations-center/functionality-health",
    enabled: true,
  },
  {
    label: "Email Workflows",
    href: "/operations-center/email-workflows",
    enabled: true,
  },
  {
    label: "Incidents",
    href: "/operations-center/incidents",
    enabled: false,
  },
  {
    label: "Audit Trail",
    href: "/operations-center/audit",
    enabled: false,
  },
] as const;

function navigationClassName({
  isActive,
}: {
  isActive: boolean;
}) {
  return [
    "flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm transition",
    isActive
      ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-fuchsia-600 text-white shadow-lg"
      : "text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-900 hover:text-white",
  ].join(" ");
}

export default function OperationsCenterSidebar() {
  return (
    <aside className="flex h-screen w-72 flex-col border-r border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-white">
      <div className="p-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">EasyChatBot</h1>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
          AI Operations Center
        </p>
      </div>

      <nav className="flex-1 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto px-3">
        {NAVIGATION_ITEMS.map((item) => (
          <NavLink
            key={item.href}
            to={item.href}
            className={navigationClassName}
          >
            {item.label}
          </NavLink>
        ))}

        <div className="my-4 border-t border-slate-800" />

        <div className="px-3 pb-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-slate-500">
          Operations Control
        </div>

        {OPERATIONS_CONTROL_ITEMS.map((item) =>
          item.enabled ? (
            <NavLink
              key={item.href}
              to={item.href}
              className={navigationClassName}
            >
              {item.label}
            </NavLink>
          ) : (
            <div
              key={item.href}
              className="flex cursor-not-allowed items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm text-slate-600"
              aria-disabled="true"
            >
              {item.label}
              <span className="ml-auto text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wide">
                Soon
              </span>
            </div>
          ),
        )}
      </nav>

      <div className="m-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/80 p-3">
        <div className="text-xs font-semibold">
          Admin Operations
        </div>
        <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">
          Controlled access
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}

/**
 * EasyChatBot AI Operations Center Shell.
 *
 * Presentation composition layer.
 *
 * Responsibilities:

 * - combine Operations Center navigation and content layout;
 * - provide the application-level presentation structure;
 * - enforce responsive shell behaviour.
 *
 * Does NOT:
 *
 * - fetch APIs;
 * - manage dashboard data;
 * - execute providers;
 * - modify Runtime;
 * - perform operational actions.
 */

import OperationsCenterLayout from "./OperationsCenterLayout";
import OperationsCenterSidebar from "./navigation/OperationsCenterSidebar";

}

  children,
}: OperationsCenterShellProps) {

      className="

        min-h-screen
        w-full
        bg-[#050816]
        text-white
      "

        className="
          hidden
          shrink-0
          lg:block
        "
      >
        <OperationsCenterSidebar />
      </aside>

      {/* Dashboard content */}
      <div
        className="
          min-w-0
          flex-1
        "
      >
        <OperationsCenterLayout>
          {children}
        </OperationsCenterLayout>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route
        path="/operations-center/governance"

        path="/privacy-governance"

  title: "Privacy Governance Center | EasyChatBot",
  description: "Read-only privacy governance oversight.",
};

=== DASHBOARD ===
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  getPrivacyGovernanceCase,
  loadPrivacyGovernanceDashboard,
} from "../../lib/privacy-governance/api";
import type {
  GovernanceFilter,
  PrivacyGovernanceCase,
  PrivacyGovernanceStatusItem,
  PrivacyGovernanceSummary,
} from "../../lib/privacy-governance/types";
import PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail from "./PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail";
import styles from "./PrivacyGovernanceDashboard.module.css";

const EMPTY_SUMMARY: PrivacyGovernanceSummary = {
  total_cases: 0,
  pending: 0,
  approved: 0,
  blocked: 0,
  approval_records: 0,
};

interface Props {
  initialLoad?: boolean;

  /**
   * Optional case ID supplied by the privacy-governance
   * deep-link route:
   *
   * /privacy-governance/:caseId
   *
   * This is intentionally dynamic. No case ID is hard-coded.
   */
  initialCaseId?: string;
}

function normalizeStatus(status: string): GovernanceFilter {
  const value = status.toUpperCase();

  if (value.includes("PENDING")) {
    return "PENDING_DPO_GRC";
  }

  if (value.includes("APPROVED")) {
    return "APPROVED";
  }

  if (
    value.includes("BLOCKED") ||
    value.includes("REJECTED")
  ) {
    return "BLOCKED";
  }

  return "ALL";
}

function statusLabel(value: string): string {
  return value.replaceAll("_", " ");
}

function formatDate(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return value;
  }

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
  }).format(date);
}

export function PrivacyGovernanceDashboard({
  initialLoad = true,
  initialCaseId,
}: Props) {
  const [summary, setSummary] =
    useState<PrivacyGovernanceSummary>(
      EMPTY_SUMMARY,
    );

  const [statusChart, setStatusChart] =
    useState<PrivacyGovernanceStatusItem[]>([]);

  const [cases, setCases] =
    useState<PrivacyGovernanceCase[]>([]);

  const [filter, setFilter] =
    useState<GovernanceFilter>("ALL");

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(initialLoad);

  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] =
    useState(false);

  const [error, setError] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  const [selectedCaseId, setSelectedCaseId] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  const [selectedCase, setSelectedCase] =
    useState<PrivacyGovernanceCase | null>(null);

  const [detailLoading, setDetailLoading] =
    useState(false);

  const [detailError, setDetailError] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  /*
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   * Dashboard data
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   */

  const loadDashboard = useCallback(
    async (
      mode: "initial" | "refresh" = "refresh",
    ) => {
      if (mode === "initial") {
        setLoading(true);
      } else {
        setRefreshing(true);
      }

      setError(null);

      try {
        const result =
          await loadPrivacyGovernanceDashboard();

        setSummary(result.summary);
        setStatusChart(result.statusChart);
        setCases(result.cases);
      } catch (cause) {
        setError(
          cause instanceof Error
            ? cause.message
            : "Unable to load privacy governance data.",
        );
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
        setRefreshing(false);
      }
    },
    [],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (initialLoad) {
      void loadDashboard("initial");
    }
  }, [initialLoad, loadDashboard]);

  /*
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   * Case filtering
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   */

  const visibleCases = useMemo(() => {
    if (filter === "ALL") {
      return cases;
    }

    return cases.filter(
      (item) =>
        normalizeStatus(
          item.governance_status,
        ) === filter,
    );
  }, [cases, filter]);

  /*
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   * Case detail
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   */

  const openCase = useCallback(
    async (caseId: string) => {
      const normalizedCaseId = caseId.trim();

      if (!normalizedCaseId) {
        setSelectedCaseId(null);
        setSelectedCase(null);
        setDetailError(
          "A valid governance case ID is required.",
        );
        return;
      }

      setSelectedCaseId(normalizedCaseId);
      setSelectedCase(null);
      setDetailError(null);
      setDetailLoading(true);

      try {
        const detail =
          await getPrivacyGovernanceCase(
            normalizedCaseId,
          );

        setSelectedCase(detail);
      } catch (cause) {
        setDetailError(
          cause instanceof Error
            ? cause.message
            : "Unable to load this governance case.",
        );
      } finally {
        setDetailLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [],
  );

  /*
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   * Privacy Governance Email Deep Link
   *
   * Reminder email:
   *
   * /privacy-governance/CASE-E2E-001
   *
   * or dynamically:
   *
   * /privacy-governance/<actual-case-id>
   *
   * The route passes the case ID to this dashboard through
   * initialCaseId.
   *
   * We then use the existing openCase() application flow.
   *
   * No case ID is hard-coded.
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   */

  useEffect(() => {
    const caseId = initialCaseId?.trim();

    if (!caseId) {
      return;
    }

    void openCase(caseId);
  }, [initialCaseId, openCase]);

  const retryCase = useCallback(() => {
    if (selectedCaseId) {
      void openCase(selectedCaseId);
    }
  }, [openCase, selectedCaseId]);

  /*
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   * Phase 6I
   *
   * Decision completed inside the case drawer.
   *
   * The backend returns the authoritative refreshed case.
   * We immediately update:
   *
   *   1. Selected case
   *   2. Case register
   *   3. Dashboard KPIs
   *   4. Status chart
   *   5. Approval record count
   *
   * The dashboard remains the same page.
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   */

  const handleDecisionComplete = useCallback(
    (updatedCase: PrivacyGovernanceCase) => {
      setSelectedCase(updatedCase);

      setCases((current) =>
        current.map((item) =>
          item.case_id === updatedCase.case_id
            ? updatedCase
            : item,
        ),
      );

      /*
       * Re-read the authoritative dashboard state.
       *
       * This is important because approval_records,
       * status counts and chart values are calculated
       * from the backend registries.
       */
      void loadDashboard("refresh");
    },
    [loadDashboard],
  );

  /*
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   * Status filtering
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   */

  const filterFromStatus = useCallback(
    (status: string) => {
      setFilter(normalizeStatus(status));
    },
    [],
  );

  /*
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   * KPI configuration
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   */

  const kpis: Array<{
    label: string;
    value: number;
    filter: GovernanceFilter;
  }> = [
    {
      label: "Total cases",
      value: summary.total_cases,
      filter: "ALL",
    },
    {
      label: "Pending DPO/GRC",
      value: summary.pending,
      filter: "PENDING_DPO_GRC",
    },
    {
      label: "Approved",
      value: summary.approved,
      filter: "APPROVED",
    },
    {
      label: "Rejected / blocked",
      value: summary.blocked,
      filter: "BLOCKED",
    },
  ];

  /*
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   * Render
   * ---------------------------------------------------------
   */

  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      {/* =====================================================
          HEADER
      ====================================================== */}

      <header className={styles.header}>
        <div>
          <p className={styles.brand}>
            EASYCHATBOT Â· GOVERNANCE
          </p>

          <h1>
            Privacy Governance Center
          </h1>

          <p className={styles.subtitle}>
            Read-only oversight of privacy-impacting
            changes, validation and human approval
            evidence.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={styles.refresh}
          onClick={() =>
            void loadDashboard("refresh")
          }
          disabled={refreshing}
        >
          {refreshing
            ? "Refreshingâ€¦"
            : "Refresh"}
        </button>
      </header>

      {/* =====================================================
          ERROR BANNER
      ====================================================== */}

      {error && (
        <section
          className={styles.errorBanner}
          role="alert"
        >
          <div>
            <strong>
              Unable to load governance data.
            </strong>

            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() =>
              void loadDashboard("refresh")
            }
          >
            Retry
          </button>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* =====================================================
          KPI CARDS
      ====================================================== */}

      <section
        className={styles.kpis}
        aria-label="Governance KPIs"
      >
        {kpis.map((kpi) => (
          <button
            type="button"
            key={kpi.label}
            className={`${styles.kpi} ${
              filter === kpi.filter
                ? styles.kpiActive
                : ""
            }`}
            onClick={() =>
              setFilter(kpi.filter)
            }
          >
            <span>{kpi.label}</span>

            <strong>
              {loading
                ? "â€¦"
                : kpi.value}
            </strong>
          </button>
        ))}
      </section>

      {/* =====================================================
          GOVERNANCE STATUS
      ====================================================== */}

      <section className={styles.panel}>
        <div className={styles.panelHeader}>
          <div>
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>
              OVERSIGHT
            </p>

            <h2>
              Governance status
            </h2>
          </div>

          <span>
            {loading
              ? "Loadingâ€¦"
              : `${summary.approval_records} approval records`}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className={styles.statusList}>
          {statusChart.length ? (
            statusChart.map((item) => {
              const maximum = Math.max(
                1,
                ...statusChart.map(
                  (entry) => entry.count,
                ),
              );

              return (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className={`${styles.statusRow} ${
                    filter ===
                    normalizeStatus(
                      item.status,
                    )
                      ? styles.statusActive
                      : ""
                  }`}
                  key={item.status}
                  onClick={() =>
                    filterFromStatus(
                      item.status,
                    )
                  }
                >
                  <span>
                    {statusLabel(
                      item.status,
                    )}
                  </span>

                  <span
                    className={
                      styles.barTrack
                    }
                  >
                    <span
                      className={styles.bar}
                      style={{
                        width: `${
                          (item.count /
                            maximum) *
                          100
                        }%`,
                      }}
                    />
                  </span>

                  <strong>
                    {item.count}
                  </strong>
                </button>
              );
            })
          ) : (
            <div
              className={
                styles.emptyState
              }
            >
              No governance status data.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =====================================================
          CASE REGISTER
      ====================================================== */}

      <section className={styles.panel}>
        <div className={styles.panelHeader}>
          <div>
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>
              CASE REGISTER
            </p>

            <h2>
              Privacy governance cases
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div
            className={
              styles.caseMeta
            }
          >
            {filter !== "ALL" && (
              <button
                type="button"
                className={
                  styles.clearFilter
                }
                onClick={() =>
                  setFilter("ALL")
                }
              >
                {statusLabel(filter)} Ã—
              </button>
            )}

            <span>
              {loading
                ? "Loadingâ€¦"
                : `${visibleCases.length} cases`}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div
          className={styles.tableWrap}
        >
          <table>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>CASE</th>
                <th>TRIGGER</th>
                <th>TRIGGERED</th>
                <th>RISK</th>
                <th>VALIDATION</th>
                <th>STATUS</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {!loading &&
                visibleCases.map(
                  (item) => (
                    <tr
                      key={item.case_id}
                    >
                      <td>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className={
                            styles.caseLink
                          }
                          onClick={() =>
                            void openCase(
                              item.case_id,
                            )
                          }
                          aria-label={`Open case ${item.case_id}`}
                        >
                          {item.case_id}
                        </button>
                      </td>

                      <td>
                        {statusLabel(
                          item.trigger_type,
                        )}
                      </td>

                      <td>
                        {formatDate(
                          item.triggered_at,
                        )}
                      </td>

                      <td>
                        {item.risk}
                      </td>

                      <td>
                        {statusLabel(
                          item.validation_status,
                        )}
                      </td>

                      <td>
                        <span
                          className={
                            styles.statusPill
                          }
                        >
                          {statusLabel(
                            item.governance_status,
                          )}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ),
                )}
            </tbody>
          </table>

          {loading && (
            <div
              className={
                styles.emptyState
              }
              role="status"
            >
              Loading governance casesâ€¦
            </div>
          )}

          {!loading &&
            !visibleCases.length && (
              <div
                className={
                  styles.emptyState
                }
              >
                {filter === "ALL"
                  ? "No governance cases are currently registered."
                  : "No cases match the selected filter."}
              </div>
            )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =====================================================
          CASE DETAIL DRAWER

          Phase 6E:
            Case detail

          Phase 6F:
            Live API/dashboard integration

          Phase 6G:
            Human governance decisions

          Phase 6I:
            Decision â†’ registry â†’ Excel â†’ refreshed UI

          Deep-link support:
            /privacy-governance/:caseId
            â†’ initialCaseId
            â†’ openCase(caseId)
            â†’ case detail
      ====================================================== */}

      <PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail
        open={Boolean(
          selectedCaseId,
        )}
        loading={detailLoading}
        error={detailError}
        caseData={selectedCase}
        onClose={() => {
          setSelectedCaseId(null);
          setSelectedCase(null);
          setDetailError(null);
        }}
        onRetry={retryCase}
        onDecisionComplete={
          handleDecisionComplete
        }
      />
    </main>
  );
}

export default PrivacyGovernanceDashboard;

=== CASE DETAIL ===
import type {
  PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail as PrivacyGovernanceCase,
} from "../../lib/privacy-governance/types";
import PrivacyGovernanceDecisionActions from "./PrivacyGovernanceDecisionActions";
import styles from "./PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail.module.css";

interface Props {
  open: boolean;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  caseData: PrivacyGovernanceCase | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  onRetry: () => void;
  onDecisionComplete: (
    updatedCase: PrivacyGovernanceCase,
  ) => void;
}

function formatDate(value?: string | null): string {
  if (!value) {
    return "â€”";
  }

  const date = new Date(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
    return value;
  }

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
  }).format(date);
}

function statusLabel(value?: string | null): string {
  if (!value) {
    return "Unknown";
  }

  return value.replaceAll("_", " ");
}

export function PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail({
  open,
  loading,
  error,
  caseData,
  onClose,
  onRetry,
  onDecisionComplete,
}: Props) {
  if (!open) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div
      className={styles.backdrop}
      role="presentation"
      onMouseDown={onClose}
    >
      <aside
        className={styles.drawer}
        aria-label="Privacy governance case detail"
        onMouseDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <header className={styles.header}>
          <div>
            <p className={styles.eyebrow}>
              CASE DETAIL
            </p>

            <h2>
              {caseData?.case_id ??
                "Privacy governance case"}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.closeButton}
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close case detail"
          >
            Ã—
          </button>
        </header>

        {loading && (
          <div
            className={styles.state}
            role="status"
          >
            Loading case detailsâ€¦
          </div>
        )}

        {!loading && error && (
          <div
            className={styles.errorState}
            role="alert"
          >
            <strong>
              Unable to load this case.
            </strong>

            <span>{error}</span>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onRetry}
            >
              Retry
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {!loading && !error && caseData && (
          <div className={styles.content}>
            {/* -------------------------------------------------
                Governance status
            ------------------------------------------------- */}

            <section className={styles.summaryCard}>
              <span>
                Governance status
              </span>

              <strong className={styles.status}>
                {statusLabel(
                  caseData.governance_status,
                )}
              </strong>
            </section>

            {/* -------------------------------------------------
                Case / Change overview
            ------------------------------------------------- */}

            <section className={styles.section}>
              <h3>Overview</h3>

              <dl className={styles.details}>
                <div>
                  <dt>Case ID</dt>
                  <dd>
                    {caseData.case_id}
                  </dd>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <dt>Change ID</dt>
                  <dd>
                    {caseData.change_id ?? "â€”"}
                  </dd>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <dt>Title</dt>
                  <dd>
                    {caseData.title ?? "â€”"}
                  </dd>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <dt>Summary</dt>
                  <dd>
                    {caseData.summary ?? "â€”"}
                  </dd>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <dt>Trigger</dt>
                  <dd>
                    {statusLabel(
                      caseData.trigger_type,
                    )}
                  </dd>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <dt>Trigger source</dt>
                  <dd>
                    {caseData.trigger_source ?? "â€”"}
                  </dd>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <dt>Triggered</dt>
                  <dd>
                    {formatDate(
                      caseData.triggered_at,
                    )}
                  </dd>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <dt>Risk</dt>
                  <dd>
                    {caseData.risk ?? "â€”"}
                  </dd>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <dt>Validation</dt>
                  <dd>
                    {statusLabel(
                      caseData.validation_status,
                    )}
                  </dd>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <dt>Email status</dt>
                  <dd>
                    {statusLabel(
                      caseData.email_status,
                    )}
                  </dd>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <dt>Final decision</dt>
                  <dd>
                    {statusLabel(
                      caseData.final_decision,
                    )}
                  </dd>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <dt>Decision time</dt>
                  <dd>
                    {formatDate(
                      caseData.final_decision_at,
                    )}
                  </dd>
                </div>
              </dl>
            </section>

            {/* -------------------------------------------------
                Approval evidence
            ------------------------------------------------- */}

            <section className={styles.section}>
              <div className={styles.sectionHeading}>
                <h3>Approval evidence</h3>

                <span>
                  {caseData.approval_records
                    ?.length ?? 0}{" "}
                  record
                  {(caseData.approval_records
                    ?.length ?? 0) === 1
                    ? ""
                    : "s"}
                </span>
              </div>

              {caseData.approval_records
                ?.length ? (
                <div className={styles.approvals}>
                  {caseData.approval_records.map(
                    (record) => (
                      <article
                        className={styles.approval}
                        key={record.approval_id}
                      >
                        <div
                          className={
                            styles.approvalTop
                          }
                        >
                          <strong>
                            {record.role}
                          </strong>

                          <span>
                            {statusLabel(
                              record.decision,
                            )}
                          </span>
                        </div>

                        <p>
                          {record.reviewer}
                        </p>

                        {record.comment && (
                          <blockquote>
                            {record.comment}
                          </blockquote>
                        )}

                        {record.decided_at && (
                          <small>
                            {formatDate(
                              record.decided_at,
                            )}
                          </small>
                        )}
                      </article>
                    ),
                  )}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <p className={styles.empty}>
                  No approval evidence has been
                  recorded for this case.
                </p>
              )}
            </section>

            {/* -------------------------------------------------
                HUMAN GOVERNANCE DECISION
                Existing Phase 6G decision component
                is now explicitly integrated into
                the Phase 6E/6F case-detail drawer.
            ------------------------------------------------- */}

            <PrivacyGovernanceDecisionActions
              caseData={caseData}
              onDecisionComplete={
                onDecisionComplete
              }
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}

export default PrivacyGovernanceCaseDetail;
